===============================================================================
A Little About This FAQ/Move List                                        Menu01
===============================================================================

This FAQ/Move List will contain information that I have collected while playing
the game.  There are many elements that are still in the game that were present
in WWE SmackDown! vs Raw 2008, but things have also been removed as well.  One
such thing is the availability of the strong Irish whip.  Now, instead of it
being available for every character, it must be earned in career mode.  A new
feature added in is the ability to do two signature moves, which if you've been
watching sports entertainment for a while like I have, is the move used to set
up for a finisher.

Also new for WWE SvR 2009 is the Road to WrestleMania, which let's you view a
few superstars' path to the grandest stage of them all.

Online mode is also only available if you have a PlayStation Network account
setup.

I have also added in a little code for each section which you can use to get to
a certain area fairly easier.  Just use Ctrl + F to search, or Open Apple + F
if you're on a Mac.

There is also a section for downloadable content.

===============================================================================
Controls                                                                 Menu02
===============================================================================

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Basic Controls                                                         Menu02.1
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------

------------------
Controls Notations
------------------

I will be using notations to represent buttons on the PlayStation controller:

D-Pad - Refers to the directional pad above the left analog stick
L - Refers to the left analog stick
R - Refers to the right analog stick
T - Refers to the triangle button
[] - Refers to the square button

--------------
Basic Controls
--------------

These are the basic controls for the game.  I will also include controls for
specialty matches in this section.  These basic controls are used in every
match type, so the specialty matches will only include controls specially
included for those match types.

D-Pad - Taunts
L - Move superstar
R - Quick grapple
T - Finisher when momentum bar is full
    Signature move when one is stored
[] - Strike attacks
O - Irish whip/Drop weapon/Pick up opponent
X - Pick up and drop weapon/Jump dwon from turnbuckle
L1 + L - Run
R1 + R up/down - Strong grapples
R1 + R left/right - Ultimate control grapples
Run + []/R1 + [] - Running strike attacks
Run + R up/down or left/right - Running grapple moves
R2 - Reverse grapple moves
L2 - Reverse strike attacks
R2 + R1 - Store signature move when momentum bar is full

------------
Ladder Match
------------

X - Pick up/Set up ladder
O - Drop ladder
L + X - Climbs set up ladder
L/R/L + R - Reach for belt/briefcase
T - Superplex on top of set up ladder with a signature move stored
R - Place opponent on ladder when carrying ladder/Perform grapple move when
    opponent is on ladder

-----------
Table Match
-----------

X - Pick up/Set up table
O - Drop table
T - Put opponent through table (drag opponent to a set up table, must have a
    Signature move stored)

----------------
Steel Cage Match
----------------

L + X - Climb wall/Exit cage
X - Climb meter (when on the wall)
[] - Hit cage wall

--------------------
Hell in a Cell Match
--------------------

X - Open cell door
L + X - Climb cell wall
R - Throw opponent off the cell (opponent must be near the edge of the roof)
O - Irish Whip opponent into cell wall (outside the cell)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Advanced Controls                                                      Menu02.2
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------

There are some added controls to WWE SmackDown! vs Raw 2009.

Defensive Stance - As you tap the buttons furiously after getting knocked down,
                   you'll notice now that below the HUD it will say "Hold L2
                   and R2".  What this button combination will do is it will
                   make your superstar roll out of harm's way as your opponent
                   tries to either grapple or strike you while you're down.
Ground and Pound - After you knock your opponent down, hold down X in order to
                   perform an ultimate control grapple.
Rope Rebound - WWE SmackDown! vs Raw 2008 first introduced the free running
               system.  In WWE SmackDown! vs Raw 2009, this is present also,
               but what's new is when you're running and rebound off the ropes,
               you no long need to hold down L1 and move the left analog stick
               in order to continue running.  Press L1 to stop running.
Dive Setup - Irish whip your opponent into a corner and run towards him.  Your
             superstar will perform a snapmare and then jump onto the top
             turnbuckle, setting up for a top rope move.
Fists of Fury - In previous WWE SmackDown! vs Raw games, you needed to connect
                with your first two strike attacks in order for the third to
                activate.  In WWE SmackDown! vs Raw 2009, newly added is what
                is called Fists of Fury.  Basically, this is your three strike
                attack combination.  It's activated by holding down R1 and
                pressing [].

===============================================================================
Environmental Grapples                                                   Menu03
===============================================================================

Present in the game are what are called environmental grapples.  These are
moves that you perform by dragging your opponent either when they are on the
ground or in a strong grapple.

------------------------------
Environmental Grapples In Ring
------------------------------

With your opponent in a strong grapple in the ring, there are three areas where
you can perform environmental grapples:

Ropes - Drag your opponent to any of the ropes to mount him and go to town.
Northwest/Southeast Turnbuckles - Drag your opponent to either of these corners
                                  to perform a mounted 10 punch.
Northeast/Southwest Turnbuckles - Drag your opponent to either of these corners
                                  to perform chops.

While your opponent is laying down, hold down R to grab and use L to drag them
to these areas:

Ropes - Grab your opponent from the upper body and drag them to the ropes to
        perform a choke.
Northeast/Southwest Turnbuckles - Your opponent must be lying face up; grab him
                                  from the lower body and drag him to either of
                                  these corners.  Hold R after the move to
                                  perform another environmental grapple.

---------------------------------------
Environmental Grapples Outside the Ring
---------------------------------------

Outside the ring, you'll have to drag your standing opponent to each of these
areas in order to perform an environmental grapple:

Metal Steps - Drag your standing opponent to the steps to slam his head into
              them.
Ring Post - Drag your standing opponent to the ring post to injure his arm.
Ring Apron - Drag your standing opponent to the ring apron to perform chops and
             rub your forearm in his face.
Security Barricade - Drag your standing opponent to the security barricade to
                     perform an environmental grapple.
Announcer Tables - Remove the coverings on the announcer tables then drag your
                   standing opponent to them to slam his head into them.

===============================================================================
Game Menus                                                               Menu04
===============================================================================

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Main Menu                                                              Menu04.1
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------

----------
Exhibition
----------

This is where you go to play with or against friends or if you want to see how
your created superstar does against the WWE Superstars.  Here is what you'll
find in this sub-menu:

------------
-One on One-
------------

Normal Match - The classic singles match.
Inferno Match - Do moves on your opponent to get the temperature to 500 degrees
                then drag your opponent to the flames to light him on fire.
First Blood - A no holds barred match where the first superstar to bleed is
              declared the loser.
Falls Count Anywhere - Pinfalls and submissions count anywhere in this match.
Hell in a Cell - Pinfalls and submissions count inside the ring in this vicious
                 match.
Ironman - The superstar with the most pinfall and submission wins when the
          timer runs out is declared the winner.
Ladder - The first superstar to retrieve the belt/briefcase hanging over the
         ring is declared the winner.
Last Man Standing - The superstar that cannot answer the ten count is declared
                    the loser.
Normal Submission - The only way to win is by making your opponent submit.
Table - Put your opponent through a table to win this match.
TLC - Using tables, ladders, or chairs is legal in this match.
Steel Cage - Escape the cage to win this match.
Backstage - There are two areas to choose from: locker room and backstage; to
            win, K.O. your opponent.
ECW Extreme Rules Match - Weapons are legal in this match, but pinfalls and
                          submissions only count inside the ring.

------------
-Two on Two-
------------

Tag Team - This is your classic tag team match.
Tornado Tag - Like a normal Tag Team match with everyone starting inside the
              ring with pinfalls and submissions only counting inside the ring.
Falls Count Anywhere Tornado Tag - Like a Tornado Tag match with pinfalls and
                                   submissions counting anywhere.
Hell in a Cell Tornado Tag - A tornado Tag Team match inside a Hell in a Cell
                             with pinfalls and submissions counting inside the
                             ring.
Ladder Tornado Tag - The first team to retrieve the belt/briefcase hanging over
                     the ring is declared the winner.
Steel Cage Tornado Tag - The first team to escape the cage wins the match.
Table Tornado Tag - The first team to put a superstar from the opposing team
                    through a table wins the match.
TLC Tornado Tag - A Tornado Tag Team match including tables, ladders, and
                  chairs with pinfalls counting inside the ring only.
Elimination Tag - The first team to either pin or get a submission on both
                  superstars of the opposing team wins the match.
ECW Extreme Rules Match Tornado Tag - Weapons are legal in this Tornado Tag
                                      team match with pinfalls and submissions
                                      counting inside the ring.

---------------
-Triple Threat-
---------------

Normal - Count outs are turned off and the only ways to win are by pinfall or
         submission.
Falls Count Anywhere - Like a normal Triple Threat match, except pinfalls or
                       submissions count anywhere.
Hell in a Cell - A triple threat match in the Hell in a Cell.
Ladder - The first superstar to retrieve the belt/briefcase that is hanging
         over the ring wins.
Steel Cage - The first superstar to escape the cage is declared the winner.
Table - The first superstar to put another superstar through a table wins.
TLC - A Triple Threat match with tables, ladders, and chairs.  The only way
      to win is by pinfall inside the ring.
ECW Extreme Rules Match - Weapons are legal in this match but pinfalls and
                          submissions only count inside the ring.

-------------
-Fatal-4-Way-
-------------

Normal - Four superstars start in the ring at once, pinfalls and submissions
         only count inside the ring.
Falls Count Anywhere - Like the Normal Fatal-4-Way match, except pinfalls and
                       submissions can be done anywhere.
Battle Royal - This is a four superstar elimination match.
Hell in a Cell - Like the Normal Fatal-4-Way match, except inside the Hell in a
                 Cell.
Ladder - The first superstar to pull down the briefcase or belt hanging above
         the ring is declared the winner.
Steel Cage - The first superstar to exit the cage is declared the winner.
Table - Put a superstar through a table to win the match.  The announcer tables
        count.
TLC - A Fatal-4-Way match including tables, ladders, and chairs.  Victory is
      achieved by pinfall.
ECW Extreme Rules Match - Weapons are legal in this match, but pinfalls and
                          submissions are only valid inside the ring.

-------
-6-Man-
-------

Battle Royal - An everyone for themselves elimination match.
Normal Tag - A regular tag team match with each team having three superstars
             each.
Elimination Tag - Like a Normal Tag match, except your team wins when each
                  member of the other team is pinned or submits.
Elimination Chamber - Two superstars start in the ring while four superstars
                      start in cells and one is released every two minutes.
                      Each superstar is eliminated until one is left.
Armageddon ell in a Cell - Six superstars start in the ring inside the cell,
                           the winning superstar pins or gets another
                           superstar to submit.
Money in the Bank - This is a six man ladder match where each superstar tries
                    to pull down a briefcase hanging over the middle of the
                    ring.

----------
-Handicap-
----------

Gauntlet - Essentially a one on three match; starts as a one on one match and
           as each opponent is pinned or submits, the next opponent enters.
One on Two - Two superstars against one with everyone in the ring all at once.
One on Tag - Like the One on Two match, but the team that has two superstars
             behave as if they're in a tag match.
One on Three - Like the One on Tag match, the team with three superstars has
               two superstars on the ring apron like they were in a tag match.
One on Trio - A three on one match with every superstar starting in the ring.
Tag on Trio - Like a tag team match with one team having one more member.

--------------
-Royal Rumble-
--------------

10-Man Royal Rumble - An over the top rope match involving a maximum of ten
                      superstars
20-Man Royal Rumble - An over the top rope match involving a maximum of twenty
                      superstars
30-Man Royal Rumble - The traditional over the top rope match involving a
                      maximum of thirty superstars.

------
My WWE
------

My WWE has three sub-menus.

---------------
 Roster Editor
---------------

This sub-menu has three sub-menus of its own:

----------------------
-Superstar Management-
----------------------

Superstar management allows you to edit brand rosters, how the fans react to
the superstars, and let's you set title holders.

-----------------
-Team Management-
-----------------

Team management allows you to edit or disband the stables that are preset and
also allows you to create new stables.

---------------
-Download Data-
---------------

This menu item becomes available after you have purcased the downloadable
content package from the PlayStation Store.  The package costs $4.99 US.

--------------------
 WWE Highlight Reel
--------------------

The highlight reel allows you to edit footage from matches that you save.  You
can then upload these short clips so that everyone can view them online.

---------
 Options
---------

The options menu sounds just like how it sounds.  This menu has sub-menus that
allow you to change the gameplay, sound volume, and auto-save among other
things.

Match Rules - Presets the rules for marches you play in exhibition.
Gameplay Settings - Allows you to adjust the difficulty level of the computer
                    controlled characters and the controller setup.
Sound Settings - Allows you to adjust the sound settings.
Save Data Manager - Go here to manually load previously saved data or manually
                    save data; you can also turn the auto-save feature on and
                    off here.
Credits - Rolls the credits for the game.
Legends of WrestleMania preview - Shows a preview for WWE Legends of
                                  WrestleMania.
Bonus WWE Matches - Shows the main event match from WrestleMania XXIV.
Cheat Codes - Input cheat codes that you find for the game here.
Exit - Takes you to the previous menu.


--------------------
Road to WrestleMania
--------------------

The Road to WrestleMania let's you view the journey through the eyes of seven
superstars from After Armageddon to the grandest stage of them all:
WrestleMania.  Going through this mode also unlocks a few things such as
arenas, playable and non-playable characters, entrances, alternate outfits, and
move sets.  You won't be able to use your created wrestle in this mode.  Here
are what you need to do to unlock things in this mode:

------------------------
 Batista & Rey Mysterio
------------------------

Batista and Rey Mysterio's path can be played as a co-op path with a friend.
If you don't have a friend, extra controller, or both, you can play each of
them separately on single player.

-----------
::Batista::
-----------

Week 5 - Win Royal Rumble
Week 7 - Spear Edge twice and win the match, unlocks playable Zack Ryder and
         Curt Hawkins
Week 9 - Have Rey Mysterio lose to Edge
Week 10 - Let Rey Mysterio get pinned, unlocks move set 1
Week 13 - Win the match - unlocks playable Mr. McMahon

There is an alternate route:

Week 9 - Help Rey Mysterio win
Week 11 - Give each opponent a finisher, unlocks Saturday Night's Main Event
          arena
Week 13 - Win the match, unlocks playable Mr. McMahon

----------------
::Rey Mysterio::
----------------

Week 4 - Pin John Morrison, unlocks Rey's Road to WrestleMania alternate
         outfit
Week 5 - Win Royal Rumble
Week 9 - Have Batista lose to Edge
Week 10 - Let Batista get pinned, unlocks move set 2
Week 13 - Win the Match, unlocks playable Mr. McMahon

There is an alternate route:

Week 9 - Help Batista win
Week 11 - Give each opponent a finisher, unlocks Saturday Night's Main Event
          arena
Week 13 - Win the match, unlocks playable Mr. McMahon

---------------
 Chris Jericho
---------------

Week 2 - Make Finlay submit in the backstage brawl
Week 3 - Pin Finlay, unlocks WCW Roster edit brand and WCW title
Week 6 - Beat Jeff Hardy without using a finisher, unlocks Old Jericho model
Week 8 - Call out Mr. Kennedy
Week 9 - Interfere in Randy Orton's match
Week 12 - Make Mr. Kennedy bleed, unlocks video of generic Code for Create an
          Entrance
Week 13 - Win the match, unlocks playable Masked Man

There is an alternate route:

Week 8 - Call out Randy Orton
Week 9 - Interfere in Randy Orton's march
Week 10 - Beat the masked man in a backstage brawl
Week 13 - Win the match, unlocks video of generic Code for Create an Entrance

---------
 CM Punk
---------

Week 2 - Put Jeff Hardy through a table, unlocks CM Punk's alternate outfit
Week 3 - Help Tommy Dreamer win
Week 7 - Turn the match into an ECW Extreme Rules match
Week 9 - Defeat Big Daddy V in less than 2 1/2 minutes, unlocks playable Layla
Week 10 - Pin John Morrison, unlocks Backstage Brawl Locker Room area
Week 12 - Select match type for WrestleMania
Week 13 - Win the match, unlocks playable Tazz

-----------
 John Cena
-----------

Week 2 - Make MVP submit, unlocks Tribute to the Troops arena
Week 7 - Give both opponents the FU, unlocks playable Tony
Week 9 - Select a backstage brawl area and K.O. William Regal
Week 10 - Put Umaga through a table
Week 11 - Make MVP bleed, unlocks playable Snitsky
Week 12 - Defeat Mr. Kennedy in less than 2 1/2 minutes, unlocks Gauntlet match
          in Exhibition
Week 13 - Win the match, unlocks Jeep Entrance for Create an Entrance

----------
 Triple H
----------

Week 1 - Pin Edge, unlocks move set 3
Week 7 - Win by pinfall, unlocks playable Jillian Hall
Week 9 - Make sure Shawn Michaels wins the match
Week 11 - Perform the Pedigree on Randy Orton three times during the match,
          unlocks D-Generation X entrance
Week 13 - Beat Shawn Michaels, unlocks Triple H's and Shawn Michaels' D-
          Generation X outfits

There is also an alternate route:

Week 9 - Make sure Randy Orton wins the match
Week 11 - Let Randy Orton get pinned
Week 12 - Beat Batista in under 2 1/2 minutes, unlocks Evolution entrance
Week 13 - Win the match, unlocks playable Ric Flair

------------
 Undertaker
------------

Week 2 - Beat Finlay in less time than it took to beat Santino Marella in week
         1, unlocks non-playable Hornswoggle
Week 5 - Tombstone Kane twice
Week 8 - Make Finlay Bleed, unlocks Brothers of Destruction entrance
Week 9 - Choose Santino Marella as your opponent and beat him in the match
Week 10 - Win the match
Week 11 - Help Rey Mysterio win the match, unlocks Santino Zombie model
Week 13 - Win the match, unlocks playable Boogeyman

There is also an alternate route:

Week 9 - Choose Finlay as your opponent and beat him in the match
Week 10 - Win the match
Week 11 - Help Rey Mysterio win the match, unlocks Finlay Zombie model
Week 13 - Win the Match, unlocks playable Boogeyman

----------
Game Modes
----------

-------------
-Career Mode-
-------------

If you made a CAW, this is the only way to raise its stats.

As you go through career mode, you start out on the Intercontinental Title
path.  In each match that you go through, you can earn up to five stars.  This
is tallied from the match that you just competed in.  The total number of
points you earned based on how the match went determines the amount of stars
you receive and you receive one star for every two hundred points you earn.
These points are then put into raising your CAW's stats.

Durability: Raises when you receive damage during a match
Strength:   Raises with strike attacks you use in a match
Speed:      Raises by running during a match
Submission: Raises by using submission moves in a match
Technical:  Raises by using grapple moves in a match
Stamina:    Raises when you win a match with a lot of stamina left
Charisma:   Raises by using taunts during a match
Hardcore:   Raises by using weapons during a match

In order to compete for the title, you must first become number one contender.
In order to become number one contender, you must accumulate a certain amount
of stars in order to qualify to issue a challenge.  In each path, you need to
accumulate a total of fifteen stars in order to be able to receive a number one
contender match.  In each number one contender match, you are automatically
put into a specialty match which unlocks when you win the match.  Here's a run
down:

----------------------
 #1 Contender Matches
----------------------

Intercontinental - Unlocks Steel cage match
ECW - Unlocks ECW Extreme Rules match
United States - Unlocks Single w/ Manager match
Cruiserweight - Unlocks Ladder match
Hardcore - Unlocks Table match
World Heavyweight - Unlocks Hell in a Cell match
WWE - Unlocks TLC match

---------------
 Title Matches
---------------

Intercontinental - Opens ECW and United States paths
ECW - Opens WWE Tag and World Tag Team paths
United States - Opens Cruiserweight and Hardcore paths
Cruiserweight - Opens WWE and World Heavyweight paths
Hardcore - Unlocks title belt

Also, new for WWE SmackDown! vs Raw 2009, you can go through career mode using
a Diva CAW.  In a Diva career, you start out on the path of the Women's Title.
To be able to qualify for a number one contender match, you need to obtain
thirteen stars.  There's not much to unlock in a Diva's career, but here's the
run down:

----------------------
 #1 Contender Matches
----------------------

Intercontinental - Unlocks Single w/ Manager match

---------------
 Title Matches
---------------

Women's - Opens Intercontinental and United States paths
Intercontinental - Opens WWE Tag, World Tag, and ECW paths
United States - Opens Cruiserweight path
Cruiserweight - Opens WWE and World Heavyweight paths

There are also attributes that your character can earn as you go through
season.  They are:

Dirty Pin: A pin attempt using the ropes for leverage.
           How to Use: Go for a pinfall while near the ropes.
           How to Get: Get a 5-star rating against 3 different opponents who
                       have this ability.

Durability: Health recovery.
            How to Use: Hold T after sustaining red limb damage.
            How to Get: Beat a Superstar rated 20 points higher overall while
                        both having orange damage.

Evasive Dodge: An evasive rolling move.
               How to Use: L2 or R2 and L.
               How to Get: Win a Special Match against 2 opponents.

Fan Favorite: Special taunt that lower's opponent's momentum.
              How to Use: Executed when your momentum is full.
              How to Get: Successfully taunt 10 times in one match (can't have
                          Steal Taunt ability).

Hardcore Resurrection: Using blood to recover your health.
                       How to Use: Press T when holding a chair.
                       How to Get: Make opponents bleed 10 times and bleed
                                   yourself 10 times (rewarded on win with
                                   blood).

Hammer Throw: Strong Irish whip.
              How to Use: Hold O when doing an Irish whip.
              How to Get: Earn an ability within your first 10 Career matches
                          then get the Sit in the Corner award.

Kip-Up: Temporary damage recovery.
        How to Use: Press T when down.
        How to Get: Win 6 championships.

K.O.: Enhances your ability to K.O. opponents.
      How to Use: Automatic when doing K.O.-enabled moves.
      How to Get: Earn 5-star ratings against every competitor in a path
                  (champ not included).

Ladder Match Specialist: Enables special ladder match actions.
                         How to Use: Automatic when climbing ladder or
                                     retrieving match prize.
                         How to Get: Get a 5 star ranked Ladder match against
                                     one of the Hardys.

Lock Pick: Instantly breaks struggle submission holds.
           How to Use: Press T while in a submission hold.
           How to Get: Face Y2J in a Singles match and escape the Walls of
                       Jericho.

Move Theft: You can steal your opponents finisher or signature move.
            How to Use: Hold R1 + T with full momentum while in opponent's
                        correct finisher position.
            How to Get: Defeat 20 different Superstars.

Object Specialist: Enables object grapples.
                   How to Use: Grapple while holding a foreign object.
                   How to Get: Execute 50 object attacks in your Career.

Outside Dives: Dive onto an out-of-ring opponent.
               How to Use: L towards out-of-ring opponent and [] while running.
               How to Get: Completed 25 dives in your Career.

Possum Pin: A sneaky pinfall, catching your opponent by surprise.
            How to Use: Automatically triggers when reversing a pin attempt.
            How to Get: Defeat 3 Superstars who have this as an ability.

Referee Shield: You can shove the ref into your opponent.
                How to Use: Press and hold X near referee.  Release to shove.
                How to Get: Get 5 DQ losses then win a championship match
                            against a clean Superstar.

Resiliency: Increased kick-out ability.
            How to Use: Automatic when being pinned.
            How to Get: Kick out of a pin attempt while having two red body
                        parts.

Springboard Dives: Enables springboard attacks to in-ring opponent.
                   How to Use: L towards rope/corner and [] button for
                               springboard attack.
                   How to Get: Win the Cruiserweight Championship.

Steal Taunt: You can steal an opponent's taunt.
             How to Use: Press R1 + D-Pad with full momentum.
             How to Get: Foil your opponent's attempt to steal your taunt
                         (cannot have Fan Favorite ability).

Steel Cage Match Specialist: Increases cage match actions.
                             How to Use: Automatic when scaling cage or once on
                                         the top.
                             How to Get: Win a Cage match in under a minute by
                                         escaping.

Submission Specialist: Opponents can tap to non-finisher submission moves.
                       How to Use: Automatic when attempting regular submission
                                   holds.
                       How to Get: Attempt 7 struggle submissions in one match
                                   and then win.

Table Match Specialist: You can stack tables.
                        How to Use: Automatic when approaching a stacked
                                    table while holding another table.
                        How to Get: Break 40 tables in your Career (announcer
                                    tables count).

As you go along in your matches, you can also receive awards depending on what
happens during the match.  Here they are:

11th Hour Save - Win a match when your partner has less than 25% health
                 remaining (tag).
4 Corners of Doom - Completed a corner grapple on all 4 corners.
A Little Pick Me Up - Picked up downed opponent 5 times.
All Heart - Take finishing moves from opponent and win.
Armed Hammer - Bust a sledgehammer on opponent.
Around the World - Executed 5 environmental grapples.
Back-Breaking Labor - Executed 7 submission moves to opponent's torso.
Blood Test - Bloodied both opponents (tag).
Blown Up - Get tired enough to fall down.
Breaking Point - Bust 4 objects.
By A THread - Win a match with less than 10% health.
Can't Catch Me - Taunt 6 times while out of the ring.
Cardio Monster - Consume 400 units of stamina.
Chain Wrestler - Completed 20 quick grapples.
Cheerleader - Pump up the crowd five times as the illegal partner (tag).
Cheese With That Whine? - Argue with the ref four times.
Continue the Pain - Manually halted the ref's pinfall count 4 times.
Could've Ended Sooner - Performed your finisher 4 times.
Crowd Can't See - Spend 30+ seconds fighting outside the ring.
Cut the Wire - Bust a barbed wire 2x4 on an opponent.
David Whups Goliath - Defeat a super heavyweight in 2 1/2 minutes with a
                      cruiserweight.
Demolition Man - Destroy all the announcer tables.
Diva Whups Goliath - Defeat a super heavyweight in 2 1/2 minutes with a diva.
Don't Call It A Comeback - Win a match with less than 50% health.
Down, Boy! - Connected with 12 strikes on downed opponent.
Drive-By Grapple - Completed 10 running grapples.
Duke the Dumpster - Bust a trash can on opponent.
Extreme Finisher - Performed 2 finishers outside the ring.
Failure to Launch - Let top-momentum timer expire 3 times.
Fear of Flying - Climb the top rope 4 times and jump down rather than
                 attacking.
Flying Around the Ring - Hit top-rope moves off every corner.
Friendly Skies - Connected with 10 attacks off the top rope.
Fuji Would Be Proud - Successfully taunted your opponent 8 times.
Get Inside Now! - STayed out of the ring until the ref's 9-count.
Greco-Roman Master - Do not use a strike the entire match.
Haymaker - Connect with 12 standing strikes.
Hey, Look At Me! - Distract referee 4 times (tag).
High-Rent District - Hit a top-rope move to an opponent at ringside.
Homage to the Dungeon - Performed 5 submission holds while out of the ring.
Impenetrable Defense - Blocked or countered at least 10 attack/grapples.
I'm Not Done Yet - Counter 2 finisher attempts.
In Control - Execute 5 total control grapples.
Insult to Injury - Successfully taunted your opponent 4 times.
Invisible Superstar - Completed match without taking damage.
Joyless Victory - Win with very little momentum.
Lock Breaker - Escaped 4 submission holds during match.
Minimalist - Only used a total of 3 different attacks or grapples for the whole
             match.
Minute Man - Win in under a minute.
Mucho Lucha - Connected with 5 attacks off the top rope.
No Cover Charge - Won without going for a single pin attempt.
No Kitchen Sink? - Struck opponent with 5 different objects.
No Seat For You! - Bust a chair on opponent.
None Shall Pass - Reverse/counter every attack/grapple in a match.
Not Paid By the Hour - Won in under 2 1/2 minutes.
Obstructed View - Spend 2 minutes fighting outside the ring.
On Strike - Do not use a s grapple the entire match.
One-Trick Pony - Use one attack or grapple exclusively for an entire match.
Out of Control - Break out of 3 total control grapples by an opponent.
Out of Stock - Come up short looking for object under ring.
Personal Space - Never touch the ropes.
Pain in the Neck - Execute 7 submission moves to an opponent's neck.
Plasma Scream - Win while both you and opponent were bloody.
Photo Opp - Did all of your superstar's signatures and finishers.
Pretzel Maker - Execute a submission move to every part of an opponent's body.
Ringland Security - Executed 2 grapples against the security barricade.
Seeing Red - Win a match after being bloodied.
Showing Mercy - Manually release 6 struggle submissions (no rope break).
Sideline Supporter - Successfully complete 4 strikes/grapples from the apron
                     (tag).
Singapore Superstar - Bust a Singapore Cane on opponent.
Sit in the Corner - Irish Whipped opponent into corner 10 times.
Speed Bag Expert - Completed 35 strikes.
Squash Match - Won with 85% of your total health.
Stay Down! - Knocked opponent down and never let them back up.
Sticks and Stones... - Win despite being hit with 3 different objects.
Stomp a Mudhole - Connect with 8 strikes with opponent in the corner.
Strong Like Bull - Executed 5 strong grapples.
Sugar Rush - Completed 10 running attacks.
Suit of Armor - Won without sustaining yellow limb damage anywhere.
Sweep the Leg - Execute 7 submission holds to an opponent's leg.
Tabletop Tapout - Lock on a submission move while opponent is on an announcer
                  table.
Tag Psychology - Perform 5 double-team moves (tag).
Take One For the Team - Win a match when you have 33% health of less and your
                        partner has 75% health or more (tag).
Take Out the Trash - Irish Whipped opponent outside ring 3 times.
Take This Broken Wing - Execute 7 submission holds to an opponent's arms.
Taunt Faster, Poser! - Have a taunt interrupted by an opponent 3 times.
The Human Autograph - Performed 5 signature moves.
There Will Be Blood - Make an opponent bleed within one minute.
Think You Overdid It? - Performed your finisher 8 times.
This is My Yard - Never left the ring.
Too Many 2s - Win by pinfall after 5 2-counts by either superstar.
Tougher Than Lumber - Win despite being put through a table (announcer tables
                      included).
Unpredictable Offense - Won while only being countered/reversed 3 or fewer
                        times.
Unsafe Workspace - Destroyed one of the announcer tables.
Well-Oiled Machine - Perform 7 double-team moves (tag).
Whipper Snapper - Irish Whipped opponent 5 times.
Wipe the Smirk Off - Broke up 3 taunt attempts by your opponent.
You'll Hurt Yourself - Take an opponent's object 3 times.

------------------
-Tournament Modes-
------------------

Tournament modes has five sub-menus.

King of the Ring - Your classic tournament style match that determines who the
                   king of the ring is.
Beat the Clock Sprint - This is a mini tournament where you have to beat your
                        opponent in the fastest time in order to win.
Quick Play Tournament - Randomly selects a tournament type for you.
Create Tournament - Create your own tournament here.
Load Tournament - Load any tournaments you created using this menu selection.

------------
Create Modes
------------

New for WWE SmackDown! vs Raw 2009 is the Create A Finisher feature.

Create A Finisher - Allows you to create your own finishing move from preset
                    moves; you can set up to ten animations for the move you
                    create.  Set this finisher in the Create A Move-Set sub-
                    menu.
Create A WWE Superstar - Allows you to create your own custom WWE Superstar.
Create An Entrance - Allows you to give your created superstar his/her own
                     entrance.  Easy creation has the basic settings; advanced
                     creation allows you to add screen color effects and pyros.
Create A Move-Set - Allows you to edit move-sets for WWE Superstars, downloaded
                    superstars, and created superstars.

------
Online
------

Just like WWE SmackDown! vs Raw 2008, you can go online and go head to head
against players from all over the world.  You need to register a PlayStation
Network account in order to play online.  Here, you'll find five submenus:

Ranked Match - These are the matches you play in in order to raise your profile
               ranking.
Player Match - You can join in on some matches to test your mettle against
               other players.  These matches don't count towards your profile
               rank.
WWE Highlight Reel - Here you can upload, download, and vote on people's videos
                     and screenshots.
My Records - All your match wins, losses, and DQs are recorded here from your
             online matches.  If you get disconnected or log off before the
             information refreshes, you won't see any updated stats.
Ranking - All the players online will be shown here in the current leader
          board.

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Match Menu                                                             Menu04.2
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Return to Game - Takes you back to the match
Restart Match - Restarts the match from the opening bell
Quit Match - Forfeits match
Camera Cuts - Changes camera angle when performing certain moves
Targeting Indicators - When on, places colored arrows above the character's
                       head to show which character you are currently focused
                       at
HUD - Shows or removes the stamina bar and character names
Display Counts - Shows or hides the counts from the referee
Control Type - Changes the controls for each controller
Match Rules - Displays the current set up for the match
Highlight Reel - Creates a film clip that you can save and share
Tutorial - Contains information about the basic controls of the game

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Roster Update 1                                                            RU01
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------

If you have set up a PlayStation Network account, you can go to the PlayStation
Store and purchase the add-on for WWE SmackDown! vs Raw 2009.  It costs a total
of $5.00 US, $4.99 plus a $0.01 processing fee.  Here's what's included:

Kane's masked outfit
Chris Jericho's new outfit
Rey Mysterio's Tribute to Eddie Guerrero outfit
Christmas outfits for Kelly Kelly and Maria

Also included are four superstars:  Charlie Haas, Evan Bourne, Super Crazy, and
Ted DiBiase.  Here are their move lists:

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Roster Update 3                                                            RU03
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Roster update number three is a free add-on, but is only available to download
if you have previously purchased and installed roster updates one and two.
This add-on allows you to use the superstars from roster updates one and two
in online play.
